Recent works showed that the problem is coNP-complete, and infinite-memory strategies are necessary in general, even in one-player games (i.e., Markov decision processes). However, memoryless strategies are sufficient for the opponent player. Despite these comprehensive results, the known algorithmic solution enumerates all memoryless strategies of the opponent, which is exponential in all cases, and does not construct a winning strategy when one exists.
  We present a recursive algorithm, based on a characterisation of the winning region, that gives a deeper insight into the problem. In particular, we show how to construct a winning strategy to achieve the combination of sure and almost-sure parity, and we derive new complexity and memory bounds for special classes of the problem, defined by fixing the index of either of the two parity conditions.

</details>


### [133] [EFX and PO Allocation Exists for Two Types of Goods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03438)
*Vladimir Davidiuk,Yuriy Dementiev,Artur Ignatiev,Danil Sagunov*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在只有两种商品类型的情况下，总能存在且能在准线性时间内计算出同时满足EFX公平性和帕累托最优（PO）的分配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在不可分物品分配中，EFX（除任一物品外的无羡慕性）分配的存在性问题，特别是在物品类别有限的情况下，如何同时保证公平性（EFX）和效率（帕累托最优，PO）。

Method: 提出了一种准线性时间复杂度（quasilinear time）的简单高效算法，用于构造同时满足EFX和PO的分配方案。

Result: 证明了当只有两种类型的商品且评价为正时，不仅存在EFX分配，还存在同时满足帕累托最优（PO）的EFX分配。这一结果改进了前人仅证明EFX存在性而未保证PO的研究。

Conclusion: 在仅有两种商品类型的情况下，满足EFX和PO的分配方案总是存在的，且可以通过高效算法求得，显著加强了该领域的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the problem of fairly and efficiently allocating indivisible goods among agents with additive valuations. We focus on envy-freeness up to any good (EFX) -- an important fairness notion in fair division of indivisible goods. A central open question in this field is whether EFX allocations always exist for any number of agents. While prior work has established EFX existence for settings with at most three distinct valuations (Prakash HV et al. 2025) and for two types of goods (Gorantla, Marwaha, and Velusamy 2023), the general case remains unresolved.
  In this paper, we extend the existent knowledge by proving that EFX allocations satisfying Pareto optimality (PO) always exist and can be computed in quasiliniear time when there are two types of goods, given that the valuations are positive. This result strengthens the existing work of (Gorantla, Marwaha, and Velusamy 2023), which only guarantees the existence of EFX allocations without ensuring Pareto optimality. Our findings demonstrate a fairly simple and efficient algorithm constructing an EFX+PO allocation.

</details>


### [134] [From No-Regret to Strategically Robust Learning in Repeated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03853)
*Junyao Zhao*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在各种拍卖机制（不仅限于一价拍卖）中，只要竞拍者对分位数表示的出价策略使用通用的无悔学习算法，就能实现战略鲁棒性，使拍卖方的平均收入上限受限于迈尔森最优拍卖收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kumar等人在2024年的研究中证明了敏捷在线梯度下降（OGD）在重复一价拍卖中具有战略鲁棒性。本研究旨在探讨这种鲁棒性是否普适，即是否适用于其他无悔学习算法以及更广泛的拍卖形式。

Method: 通过一个简单的关联公式，将迈尔森（Myerson）拍卖理论与标准无悔学习理论联系起来。研究者将出价策略表示为分位数空间上的连续区间，并将梯度反馈提供给通用的无悔学习算法（如MWU）。

Result: 1. 证明了战略鲁棒性并非OGD特有，任何基于分位数表示的无悔学习算法都具备此特性；2. 该结论适用于随时间变化的多种拍卖格式（需满足单调性和自愿参与）；3. 证明了乘法权更新（MWU）能够同时实现最优悔度保证和目前已知的最佳战略鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: 证明了在满足分配单调性和自愿参与的任何拍卖机制下，通过分位数表示法应用任何无悔学习算法都具有战略鲁棒性。这种方法无需显式最小化交换悔度（swap regret），展示了将标准悔度保证转化为复杂博弈中战略鲁棒性的潜力。

Abstract: In Bayesian single-item auctions, a monotone bidding strategy--one that prescribes a higher bid for a higher value type--can be equivalently represented as a partition of the quantile space into consecutive intervals corresponding to increasing bids. Kumar et al. (2024) prove that agile online gradient descent (OGD), when used to update a monotone bidding strategy through its quantile representation, is strategically robust in repeated first-price auctions: when all bidders employ agile OGD in this way, the auctioneer's average revenue per round is at most the revenue of Myerson's optimal auction, regardless of how she adjusts the reserve price over time.
  In this work, we show that this strategic robustness guarantee is not unique to agile OGD or to the first-price auction: any no-regret learning algorithm, when fed gradient feedback with respect to the quantile representation, is strategically robust, even if the auction format changes every round, provided the format satisfies allocation monotonicity and voluntary participation. In particular, the multiplicative weights update (MWU) algorithm simultaneously achieves the optimal regret guarantee and the best-known strategic robustness guarantee. At a technical level, our results are established via a simple relation that bridges Myerson's auction theory and standard no-regret learning theory. This showcases the potential of translating standard regret guarantees into strategic robustness guarantees for specific games, without explicitly minimizing any form of swap regret.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Online Learning with Limited Information in the Sliding Window Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03533)
*Vladimir Braverman,Sumegha Garg,Chen Wang,David P. Woodruff,Samson Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在内存受限（polylog级别）的滑动窗口模型下，仅需少量专家查询即可实现近最优遗憾值的专家预测算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式专家问题研究缺乏对“滑动窗口”场景的关注，而现实应用（如流量分析、金融交易）中近期数据往往比旧数据更重要，且内存受限。

Method: 通过限制每日仅查询2名专家的预测，结合滑动窗口（Sliding Window）模型和仅polylog(nT)比特的内存空间，设计高效的算法。

Result: 在滑动窗口和任意区间内均达到 $\sqrt{n|I|}\text{polylog}(nT)$ 的近最优遗憾值；在Bandit设置下，首次实现亚线性遗憾值，且在特定条件下可达最优遗憾值。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在受限资源下（极小查询次数与内存），依然可以实现实时动态数据的近最优专家预测，为流式算法提供了更强的理论保障。

Abstract: Motivated by recent work on the experts problem in the streaming model, we consider the experts problem in the sliding window model. The sliding window model is a well-studied model that captures applications such as traffic monitoring, epidemic tracking, and automated trading, where recent information is more valuable than older data. Formally, we have $n$ experts, $T$ days, the ability to query the predictions of $q$ experts on each day, a limited amount of memory, and should achieve the (near-)optimal regret $\sqrt{nW}\text{polylog}(nT)$ regret over any window of the last $W$ days. While it is impossible to achieve such regret with $1$ query, we show that with $2$ queries we can achieve such regret and with only $\text{polylog}(nT)$ bits of memory. Not only are our algorithms optimal for sliding windows, but we also show for every interval $\mathcal{I}$ of days that we achieve $\sqrt{n|\mathcal{I}|}\text{polylog}(nT)$ regret with $2$ queries and only $\text{polylog}(nT)$ bits of memory, providing an exponential improvement on the memory of previous interval regret algorithms. Building upon these techniques, we address the bandit problem in data streams, where $q=1$, achieving $n T^{2/3}\text{polylog}(T)$ regret with $\text{polylog}(nT)$ memory, which is the first sublinear regret in the streaming model in the bandit setting with polylogarithmic memory; this can be further improved to the optimal $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{nT})$ regret if the best expert's losses are in a random order.

</details>


### [136] [A Theoretical and Empirical Taxonomy of Imbalance in Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04149)
*Rose Yvette Bandolo Essomba,Ernest Fokoué*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基于三个基本标量 $(η, κ, Δ)$ 的理论框架，统一解释并精准预测了类别不平衡在高维数据中导致分类性能退化的几何规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管类别不平衡严重影响分类性能，但目前缺乏从统一的理论视角对其影响进行深入分析。

Method: 提出了由不平衡系数 ($η$)、样本-维度比 ($κ$) 和内在可分性 ($Δ$) 组成的理论框架。基于高斯贝叶斯分类器推导了闭式贝叶斯误差，并在高维基因组数据集上进行了不同模型的实验验证。

Result: 实验证实性能退化遵循理论预测：当 $\log(η)$ 超过 $Δ\sqrtκ$ 时，少数类召回率会崩溃。研究定义了四个性能演化阶段：正常、轻微、极端和灾难性。

Conclusion: 类别不平衡导致性能下降的本质是由三个物理量构成的几何约束。通过这三个参数，可以提前预判模型在特定任务中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Class imbalance significantly degrades classification performance, yet its effects are rarely analyzed from a unified theoretical perspective. We propose a principled framework based on three fundamental scales: the imbalance coefficient $η$, the sample--dimension ratio $κ$, and the intrinsic separability $Δ$. Starting from the Gaussian Bayes classifier, we derive closed-form Bayes errors and show how imbalance shifts the discriminant boundary, yielding a deterioration slope that predicts four regimes: Normal, Mild, Extreme, and Catastrophic. Using a balanced high-dimensional genomic dataset, we vary only $η$ while keeping $κ$ and $Δ$ fixed. Across parametric and non-parametric models, empirical degradation closely follows theoretical predictions: minority Recall collapses once $\log(η)$ exceeds $Δ\sqrtκ$, Precision increases asymmetrically, and F1-score and PR-AUC decline in line with the predicted regimes. These results show that the triplet $(η,κ,Δ)$ provides a model-agnostic, geometrically grounded explanation of imbalance-induced deterioration.

</details>
